I’ve had stretches where I felt low and not myself, but I haven’t kept track well enough to know if it was two weeks or more.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of two weeks or more where you felt sad or empty most of the time?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I’ve felt empty or kind of low at times, but I don’t really know if it was most of the time for two weeks straight. I haven’t paid close enough attention to it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt sad or empty for most of every day for two weeks or more?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know. I can remember feeling empty or down, but whether it was most of every day for two weeks or more… I just haven’t kept track like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you felt sad or empty for most of every day?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I’ve had times where I felt kind of empty or down during the day, but I can’t say it was most of every day. I just don’t track it that closely.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt sad or empty for most of every day for two weeks or more in a row?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I’ve had stretches where I felt pretty empty or low, but I can’t say for sure it was most of every day for two weeks straight.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you felt sad or empty nearly every day?\n\nPatient: I can’t say for sure. I’ve had days where I felt empty or down, but whether it was nearly every day… I just don’t really know.\n\nDoctor: When you feel really down or empty, does it usually happen after one of those times when you're feeling extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so, sometimes. I’ll wake up some mornings feeling like the whole valley’s opening up for me, just grinning for no real reason. Then if one of the hired hands questions me, or my wife tells me to slow down, I can snap real fast, like they’ve insulted me or my family.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel down or empty after those times when you have a lot of energy and feel extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure it’s that regular. Sometimes I come down hard after being all wound up and feeling good, but I haven’t paid close enough attention to say it usually happens that way.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling down or empty right after those times when you have a lot of energy and feel extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s right after. Sometimes I notice a drop later on, but I haven’t really kept track of the timing that closely.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling down or empty immediately after those times when you have a lot of energy and feel extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: I really can’t say it’s immediate. I haven’t noticed a clear pattern like that. Sometimes I feel lower after, but I don’t know if it’s right away or just later when things settle down.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling down or empty within a day or two after those times when you have a lot of energy and feel extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure about within a day or two. I haven’t really tracked it that closely. I just know sometimes I feel lower after those high-energy times, but I can’t say exactly when it starts.\n\nDoctor: Do your periods of feeling down or empty usually happen after the times when you feel extremely happy or excited?\n\nPatient: I can’t really say they usually happen after. Sometimes it feels like I come down later, but I haven’t noticed a clear order to it.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
